=== add REVIEW-LOOP-SPEC.md
new 07a75ecf1dbf5b8d79a9eace
+# The review loop ("Checking my work")
+
+Experimental. Tagged `before-review-loop` (build 2759) before it went in.
+
+The developer, 2026-11-06: the most effective way to use the agent was to have it plan, ask the side agent to
+evaluate the plan, give that answer back, repeat until the side agent agreed, then have it do the work and repeat
+the same until the side agent signed off. This builds that loop in.
+
+## When it runs
+
+The agent says it is done, with the text the developer should see if the review finds nothing:
+
+| Gate | How the agent says it is done | What the developer sees on a pass |
+|---|---|---|
+| a fix's plan (its reproduction, analysis and proposal) | `done({ kind: "plan", show })` | `show`: the plan, asking them to certify it |
+| a feature's plan, before it is built | `done({ kind: "design", show })` | nothing: the agent is told to build it (a feature's approach needs no approval - the developer, 2026-10-22) |
+| a change, tested | `commit({ ..., certify })` | `certify`: the change and its tests, asking them to certify it |
+| a diagnosis given as an answer | `done({ kind: "diagnosis", show })` | `show`: the answer |
+| before something destructive or hard to undo | `done({ kind: "risky", show })` | nothing: the agent is told to go ahead |
+
+A gate is started by the call, never by reading the agent's words.
+
+Every commit is checked and certified, whatever its complexity. Until build 2775 a commit rated 1-2 that touched one
+file and 30 lines or fewer skipped both; live, a certified one-line fix went in that way, unchecked and never
+certified. The developer, 2026-11-07: "the idea behind it was to not loop if the reviewer is not sure it's adding
+value or if it's nitpicking. the normal certification from the user/commit is still needed." Not looping on small
+things is the gate's own bar: a low-impact or unsure finding counts as a pass and goes to a Heads up box.
+
+## The reviewer
+
+A sub-agent with its own conversation, run: true (it runs tests and the program to check a claim). It reads the
+main conversation with `mainChat()`, as the side chat's agent does, and gets the agent's brief, what the agent will
+show (or claim to) the developer, and for a change the diff. It writes in the first person, and its steps are shown at the top level of the main chat as the agent's own
+(`check-step` events): a line where the check starts, its notes and snippets, and a line with what it came to - it
+reads as the agent checking its own work, not a second agent (it is not in the sub-agents list). None of it reaches
+the main agent. While it runs, the working line says "Checking my work…".
+
+It ends with one line:
+
+    REVIEW: {"verdict": "pass" | "retry" | "give-up", "findings": [...], "notLooping": "...", "headsUp": "..."}
+
+Each finding: `{ "what", "impact": "low" | "medium" | "high", "confidence": "low" | "high", "evidence" }`.
+Confidence counts as high only with evidence: a file and line, or something it ran and what that showed.
+
+## The gate
+
+| Impact | Low confidence | High confidence |
+|---|---|---|
+| low | passes | passes |
+| medium | passes | returned to the agent |
+| high | returned, marked "this may be an issue - we're not sure; check to be sure" | returned |
+
+- The scores decide: no finding in a returned cell, the review passed (whatever the verdict).
+- Findings in a returned cell go back - and from round 3 on only with `notLooping` ("this is not looping because..."):
+  without it the check FAILS (the developer, 2026-11-06: "if it can't find a reason it's not looping, it should fail
+  instead of retry"; "allow 2 send backs w/o loop reasons"). A failure: the findings go to a Heads up box, not the agent.
+- `give-up` (or an answer that cannot be read): a Heads up box with its text and its findings; the agent's text is shown.
+
+What is below the bar is never thrown away: it goes to a Heads up box of its own, the developer's to use or not
+("instead of throwing away a response if it's low impact or low confidence, let's show it as a heads up").
+
+## The loop
+
+Findings returned go to the main agent as a message of their own (shown in the chat). It fixes what holds up and says
+done again (done() or commit()), and the review runs again: round 2, 3, ... A later round is given the earlier
+rounds' findings and judges whether each was dealt with.
+
+After round 3 the loop does not stop - the developer is brought in: the findings that would have gone back go to a
+Heads up box instead (with "already checked 3 times" in it), and the agent's text is shown. The developer acts on it
+(adds it to the conversation) or dismisses it.
+
+A round counts within one piece of work: the developer's message it was asked in (one more developer message after
+findings still counts, as the commit review did).
+
+## Storage
+
+Everything is in conversation.jsonl as typed events; the request to the model and the screen each take the types
+that are theirs:
+
+| Event | Screen | Model |
+|---|---|---|
+| `work-check` (agentId, kind, round, state, returned, passed, note) | the line where a check starts | - |
+| `check-step` (agentId, event: one of the reviewer's own events) | that event, as the agent's own row | - |
+| `work-check-done` (of, state, counts) | the line with what it came to | - |
+| `user-message` with `source: "review"` (the findings returned) | a findings row | yes, as a message to the agent |
+| `assistant-text` (the agent's `show`/`certify` text on a pass) | the agent's reply | yes |
+| `heads-up` (review notes) | the Heads up box | - |
+
+The reviewer's own conversation is also kept with the sub-agents.
+
+## A restart while a check runs
+
+The check is gone with the process. When the conversation is restored its 'work-check' is marked interrupted, and
+the agent's text (kept on the event) is shown unchecked - for a risky step or a feature's plan, a Heads up box
+instead, since the agent waits on it. The reviewer is not taken up again with the other sub-agents, and a report
+it left is dropped: resumed, it reached the main agent as an ordinary sub-agent's report.
+
+## Rounds
+
+A check continues a piece of work (round 2, 3...) only when the developer has written nothing since its findings
+reached the agent. Asked for something else after them, it is new work, round 1.
+

+  static long autoRestartMs() {
+    try { String v = System.getenv("CB_SANDBOX_AUTO_RESTART_MS"); if (v != null && !v.trim().isEmpty()) return Math.max(1000L, Long.parseLong(v.trim())); } catch (NumberFormatException ignore) { }

+  static String randomKey() {
+    byte[] rnd = new byte[18];
+    new java.security.SecureRandom().nextBytes(rnd);
+    StringBuilder t = new StringBuilder();
+    for (byte b : rnd) t.append(String.format("%02x", b & 0xFF));
+    return t.toString();
+  }

+    if (key == null || key.isEmpty()) return null;

+export function applyCommits(repoDir, files, commits) {
+  const steps = gcCommitSteps(files, commits);
+  for (let n = steps.next(); ; ) {
+    if (n.done) return n.value;
+    const [args, input] = n.value;
+    n = steps.next(git(repoDir, args, input));
+  }
+}
+// THE COMMIT WITHOUT HOLDING THE SERVER (prod, 2026-11-06: code_boss stopped answering - its window disconnected, the
+// container manager said "not answering"). In the container git is JGit, a JVM started for every call, and the commit
+// ran them with spawnSync on the server's one thread: a slow one held every window that long, one that never returned
+// held them for good. Each call here is a child process like the reads above, with a time limit; one that runs past
+// it is ended and its commit fails saying so - and the steps stop there, the index left as git left it.
+export const GC_CALL_LIMIT_MS = 120000;
+function gcGitRun(repoDir, args, input, jgit, limitMs) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    let out = '', err = '', done = false, timer = null;
+    const finish = (status, timedOut = false) => { if (done) return; done = true; if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ status, stdout: out, stderr: err, timedOut }); };
+    try {
+      const child = spawn('git', gcArgs(repoDir, args, jgit), { windowsHide: true, stdio: ['pipe', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+      child.stdout.setEncoding('utf8'); child.stderr.setEncoding('utf8');
+      child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { if (out.length < 64 * 1024 * 1024) out += d; });
+      child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { if (err.length < 1024 * 1024) err += d; });
+      child.on('error', (e) => { err += String(e && e.message || e); finish(null); });
+      child.on('close', (code) => finish(code));
+      child.stdin.on('error', () => {});
+      child.stdin.end(input == null ? '' : input);
+      timer = setTimeout(() => { try { child.kill(); } catch { /* gone */ } finish(null, true); }, limitMs);
+    } catch (e) { err += String(e && e.message || e); finish(null); }
+  });
+}
+export async function applyCommitsAsync(repoDir, files, commits, { limitMs = GC_CALL_LIMIT_MS } = {}) {
+  const jgit = await gcIsJgit();
+  const steps = gcCommitSteps(files, commits);
+  for (let n = steps.next(); ; ) {
+    if (n.done) return n.value;
+    const [args, input] = n.value;
+    const r = await gcGitRun(repoDir, args, input, jgit, limitMs);
+    n = r.timedOut
+      ? steps.throw(new GcStopped(`git ${args[0]} did not finish in ${Math.round(limitMs / 1000)} s and was stopped - if git now says the index is locked, the .git/index.lock it left behind has to be deleted`))
+      : steps.next(r);
+  }
@@ 204 -0 +3

+// ONE COMMIT AT A TIME (the developer, 2026-11-06: "we ask it for 1 commit at a time and then if there's still files that
+// weren't covered, we ask again. That way the user doesn't have to wait minutes to see even the first commit"). The
+// units are the ones no commit has taken yet; `planned` the commits already suggested, so the next one follows them.
+export function buildNextCommitPrompt({ files = [], untracked = [], conversation = '', repoName = '', planned = [] } = {}) {
+  const [msg] = buildCommitRecPrompt({ files, untracked, conversation, repoName });
+  const lines = msg.content.split('\n');
+  // (the whole-plan ask and its reply format, at the end, replaced by the next-commit ask)
+  while (lines.length && !/^Group the units into commits/.test(lines[lines.length - 1])) lines.pop();
+  lines.pop();

+    lines.push('ALREADY PLANNED (earlier commits in the sequence - their units are not listed above):');
+    for (const c of planned) lines.push(`  - ${c.message}`);
+    lines.push('');
+  }
+  lines.push(`Choose ONLY the NEXT commit of a clean, logical sequence over the units listed above${planned.length ? ', following the ones already planned' : ''}: the units that belong together in it, and its message. A file's hunks may go in different commits when they are unrelated; a dependency comes before what uses it. If everything left belongs in one commit, take it all. +  lines.push('Reply with ONLY a JSON object, no prose: {"message":"...","units":["path#0","other.js"]}');
+  return [{ role: 'user', content: lines.join('\n') }];
+}
+// The next commit from the reply: { commit: { message, units } | null, error }. Known unit ids only (`validIds`: the ones
+// still left); an array is taken for its first commit.
+export function parseNextCommitRec(text, validIds) {
+  const valid = new Set(validIds || []);
+  const s = String(text || '');
+  const fence = s.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```/i);
+  let body = fence ? fence[1] : s;
+  let o = null;
+  try { o = JSON.parse(body.trim()); } catch {
+    const a = body.indexOf('{'), b = body.lastIndexOf('}');
+    if (a >= 0 && b > a) { try { o = JSON.parse(body.slice(a, b + 1)); } catch { o = null; } }
+  }
+  // (an answer with more than one: the first that still has units left - a model that gives the whole plan anyway)
+  if (Array.isArray(o)) o = o.find((c) => c && Array.isArray(c.units) && c.units.some((u) => valid.has(u))) || o[0];
+  if (!o || typeof o !== 'object') return { commit: null, error: 'model did not return a JSON object' };
+  const message = (typeof o.message === 'string' ? o.message : '').trim();
+  const units = [...new Set((Array.isArray(o.units) ? o.units : []).filter((u) => valid.has(u)))];
+  if (!message || !units.length) return { commit: null, error: 'no valid commit parsed' };
+  return { commit: { message, units }, error: null };

=== add src/agent/held-watch.mjs
new a6b64c5886c153a5c7b11517
+/**
+ * WHAT HELD THE SERVER (prod, 2026-11-06: a window said the server was disconnected and the container manager said
+ * code_boss was not answering - and nothing said what it was doing). The server has one thread; while something holds
+ * it, it cannot log anything itself (server.mjs's stall line is written only once the hold is over, and a hold that
+ * never ends writes nothing). This watcher is a second thread. The server's thread stamps a shared clock every second
+ * and the last request it took; when the clock stops for HW_HELD_MS, the watcher writes to diag.log how long, that
+ * request, and the server thread's JavaScript stack - taken through node's own inspector, in this process (no port is
+ * opened, nothing is started). A thread held inside a call that waits outside JavaScript (a child process run
+ * synchronously, a file read that does not return) cannot be paused there: the line says so, and when it came back, if
+ * it does (the request it was serving is then what names it).
+ *
+ * Only function names, file names and line numbers, and the request's method and path: never the conversation.
+ */
+import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
+
+const HW_DOING_BYTES = 240;
+
+// (the watcher thread: plain CommonJS, run from this text - it has nothing of the bundle; indented, so the build's
+// check of the bundle's one flat scope does not take its names for the bundle's)
+const HW_WORKER = String.raw`
+  const { workerData } = require('node:worker_threads');
+  const fs = require('node:fs');
+  const inspector = require('node:inspector');
+  const { sab, diagPath, heldMs, build, pid } = workerData;
+  const beat = new Float64Array(sab, 0, 1), len = new Int32Array(sab, 8, 1), doing = new Uint8Array(sab, 12);
+  const say = (line) => { try { fs.appendFileSync(diagPath, new Date().toISOString() + '  WARN  [held] ' + line + '\n', 'utf8'); } catch {} };
+  const doingNow = () => { try { const n = Math.max(0, Math.min(len[0], doing.length)); return Buffer.from(doing.slice(0, n)).toString('utf8') || '-'; } catch { return '-'; } };
+  const secs = (ms) => Math.round(ms / 1000) + 's';
+  let session = null, reportedFor = 0, waiting = null;
+  const scripts = new Map();   // scriptId -> its file (a paused frame names its script by id)
+  function frames(callFrames) {
+    return (callFrames || []).slice(0, 14).map((f) => {
+      const loc = f.location || {};
+      const u = String(scripts.get(loc.scriptId) || f.url || '').replace(/^file:\/\/\/?/, '').replace(/[?#].*$/, '').split(/[\\/]/).pop() || '?';   // (a plugin's file carries ?v=)
+      return (f.functionName || '(anonymous)') + ' ' + u + ':' + (loc.lineNumber + 1);
+    }).join(' < ');
+  }
+  function stack(heldSince) {
+    try {
+      if (!session) {
+        session = new inspector.Session(); session.connectToMainThread();
+        session.on('Debugger.scriptParsed', (m) => { try { if (m.params && m.params.url) scripts.set(m.params.scriptId, m.params.url); } catch {} });
+      }
+      let got = false;
+      session.once('Debugger.paused', (m) => {
+        got = true;
+        // paused only once it came back: where it is NOW is not what held it - that it came back, and when, is what is known
+        const late = waiting && Date.now() - waiting > 4000;
+        say(late ? 'it came back to JavaScript ' + secs(Date.now() - heldSince) + ' after the hold began' : 'its stack: ' + frames(m.params && m.params.callFrames));
+        try { session.post('Debugger.resume', () => { try { session.post('Debugger.disable'); } catch {} }); } catch {}
+        waiting = null;
+      });
+      waiting = Date.now();
+      session.post('Debugger.enable', () => {});
+      session.post('Debugger.pause', () => {});
+      setTimeout(() => { if (!got) say('it is inside a call that waits outside JavaScript (a child process run synchronously, a file read or a lock that does not return) - it cannot be paused there; its stack is written when it comes back, if it does'); }, 4000);
+    } catch (e) { say('its stack could not be taken: ' + String(e && e.message || e).slice(0, 200)); }
+  }
+  setInterval(() => {
+    const at = beat[0];
+    if (!at) return;
+    const held = Date.now() - at;
+    if (held < heldMs || reportedFor === at) return;
+    reportedFor = at;
+    say('the server has not run for ' + secs(held) + ' (build ' + build + ', pid ' + pid + ') - the last request it took: ' + doingNow());
+    if (!waiting) stack(at);
+  }, 1000);
+`;
+
+/**
+ * Start the watcher. Returns { beat(), doing(text), stop() }: beat() is called by the server's thread every second,
+ * doing() with what it is serving (a request's method and path). A watcher that cannot start returns no-ops.
+ */
+export function startHeldWatch({ diagPath, heldMs = Number(process.env.CB_HELD_MS) || 20000, build = '', disabled = /^(0|off|false|no)$/i.test(process.env.CB_HELD_WATCH || '') } = {}) {
+  const none = { beat() {}, doing() {}, stop() {} };
+  if (disabled || !diagPath) return none;
+  let w;
+  const sab = new SharedArrayBuffer(12 + HW_DOING_BYTES);
+  const beatArr = new Float64Array(sab, 0, 1), lenArr = new Int32Array(sab, 8, 1), doingArr = new Uint8Array(sab, 12);
+  try {
+    w = new Worker(HW_WORKER, { eval: true, workerData: { sab, diagPath, heldMs, build: String(build || '-'), pid: process.pid } });
+    w.unref();
+    w.on('error', () => {});
+  } catch { return none; }
+  beatArr[0] = Date.now();
+  return {
+    beat() { beatArr[0] = Date.now(); },
+    doing(text) {
+      try { const b = Buffer.from(String(text || '').slice(0, HW_DOING_BYTES), 'utf8').subarray(0, HW_DOING_BYTES); doingArr.set(b); lenArr[0] = b.length; } catch {}
+    },
+    stop() { try { w.terminate(); } catch {} },
+  };
+}

+/**
+ * THE REVIEW LOOP - "Checking my work" (REVIEW-LOOP-SPEC.md; the developer, 2026-11-06: the agent plans, the side
+ * agent evaluates, back and forth until it agrees; then the work, and the same until it signs off - "there should be a
+ * better way to build this in"). The parts with no server in them: what the reviewer is asked, how its answer is
+ * read, which findings go back to the agent, and the words of what is shown.
+ */
+
+export const REVIEW_KINDS = ['plan', 'design', 'change', 'diagnosis', 'risky'];
+/** Rounds the agent is sent findings for; past them the developer is brought in (a Heads up box). */
+export const REVIEW_ROUNDS_TO_AGENT = 3;
+/** Rounds that may send findings back without saying why it is not looping (the developer, 2026-11-06: "maybe allow 2
+ *  send backs w/o loop reasons" - a first check has no loop to explain). From the next one on the reason is required. */
+export const REVIEW_FREE_ROUNDS = 2;

+// NO "THE AGENT" IN WHAT THE CHECK IS TOLD (live, 2026-11-07: a task that said "the agent" fifteen times and "never write
+// the agent" once got "The agent's plan was presented as..." back, in a box meant to read as the agent's own)
+const KIND_WHAT = {
+  plan: 'a PLAN about to go to the developer to certify: for a fix, the reproduction, the analysis of the cause and the fix proposed; for a feature, what will be built and how',
+  design: 'a PLAN for a feature, about to be built (it is built when this check passes; the developer does not approve the approach)',
+  change: 'a CHANGE made and tested, about to go to the developer to certify',
+  diagnosis: 'a DIAGNOSIS about to be given to the developer as an answer: what causes something',
+  risky: 'a STEP about to be taken that is destructive or hard to undo',
+};
+const KIND_LOOK = {
+  // (the developer, 2026-11-07: "if it's a plan to fix an issue, that the issue has been reproduced before sending a
+  // plan to the user")
+  plan: 'FOR A FIX, THE PROBLEM MUST HAVE BEEN REPRODUCED before the plan goes to the developer. +  diagnosis: 'Is the cause shown, by something run or read, or only assumed? Is there a simpler explanation that was not ruled out? Does the evidence it gives actually support it?',

+  lines.push('');
+  lines.push('END YOUR REPORT WITH ONE LINE, on its own, exactly this shape (JSON after "REVIEW: "):');

+    ? `- verdict "retry": what you found is fixed, and the work comes back to be checked again. +  lines.push('- verdict "give-up": you cannot review this, should not go on (the work is not what was asked at all, you cannot reach what you need), or it is going round in circles. Say it in headsUp, to the developer ("I keep coming back to the same gap, so I am stopping here: ...").');

+const IMPACTS = new Set(['low', 'medium', 'high']);
+const CONFS = new Set(['low', 'high']);
+/** The reviewer's last REVIEW: line, read: { verdict, findings, notLooping, headsUp } or { error }. */
+export function parseReview(text) {
+  const s = String(text || '');
+  const lines = s.split(/\r?\n/);
+  for (let i = lines.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+    const m = /^\s*REVIEW:\s*(\{.*?)\s*$/.exec(lines[i]);
+    if (!m) continue;
+    let o; try { o = JSON.parse(m[1]); } catch { return { error: 'its REVIEW line is not JSON' }; }
+    const verdict = String(o.verdict || '').toLowerCase();
+    if (!['pass', 'retry', 'give-up'].includes(verdict)) return { error: `its verdict "${o.verdict}" is not pass, retry or give-up` };
+    const findings = (Array.isArray(o.findings) ? o.findings : []).filter((f) => f && String(f.what || '').trim()).map((f) => {
+      const evidence = String(f.evidence || '').trim();
+      const impact = IMPACTS.has(String(f.impact || '').toLowerCase()) ? String(f.impact).toLowerCase() : 'medium';
+      // high only with evidence (REVIEW-LOOP-SPEC.md: a model's own confidence is not calibrated)
+      const confidence = CONFS.has(String(f.confidence || '').toLowerCase()) && String(f.confidence).toLowerCase() === 'high' && evidence ? 'high' : 'low';
+      return { what: String(f.what).trim(), impact, confidence, evidence };
+    });
+    return { verdict, findings, notLooping: String(o.notLooping || '').trim(), headsUp: String(o.headsUp || '').trim() };
+  }
+  return { error: 'it ended without a REVIEW line' };
+}
+
+/** One finding through the gate: 'pass', 'return', or 'return-unsure' (high impact, low confidence). */
+export function gateOf(f) {
+  if (f.impact === 'high') return f.confidence === 'high' ? 'return' : 'return-unsure';
+  if (f.impact === 'medium' && f.confidence === 'high') return 'return';
+  return 'pass';
+}
+
+/**
+ * What the review comes to: { outcome: 'pass' | 'return' | 'give-up', returned: [findings to go back], passed: [below
+ * the bar - the developer's, in a Heads up box, never the agent's], note } - `note`: why it is not looping (a return),
+ * why it gave up.
+ * NOT LOOPING (the developer, 2026-11-06: "if the review agent tells the main agent to retry, it needs to say 'this is
+ * not looping because...' if it can't find a reason it's not looping, it should fail instead of retry"): findings that
+ * would go back with no notLooping are a failure - the developer gets them, the agent does not.
+ */
+export function decide(review, { round = 1 } = {}) {
+  // (the notes are the developer's, in the agent's voice - the developer, 2026-11-07: "if there's feedback ... it reads
+  // like it's the agent either talking to the user or thinking out loud")
+  if (!review || review.error) return { outcome: 'give-up', returned: [], passed: [], note: `My check came to no result I could read (${review ? review.error : 'no answer'}) - its steps are under the "Checked my work" line.` };
+  if (review.verdict === 'give-up') return { outcome: 'give-up', returned: [], passed: review.findings, note: review.headsUp || 'I stopped checking without noting why - my steps are under the "Checked my work" line.' };
+  const gated = review.findings.map((f) => ({ ...f, gate: gateOf(f) }));
+  const toReturn = gated.filter((f) => f.gate !== 'pass').map((f) => ({ ...f, unsure: f.gate === 'return-unsure' }));
+  const passed = gated.filter((f) => f.gate === 'pass');
+  if (!toReturn.length) return { outcome: 'pass', returned: [], passed, note: '' };
+  if (!review.notLooping && round > REVIEW_FREE_ROUNDS) return { outcome: 'give-up', returned: [], passed: [...toReturn, ...passed], note: `I would go back over it again, but I could not say why another round would settle it rather than go round in circles - so I am leaving it with you.` };
+  return { outcome: 'return', returned: toReturn, passed, note: review.notLooping || '' };
+}
+
+const fLine = (f) => `- ${f.unsure ? 'This may be an issue - we are not sure; check to be sure: ' : ''}${f.what} (impact ${f.impact}, confidence ${f.confidence})${f.evidence ? `\n  Evidence: ${f.evidence}` : ''}`;
+/** The message the main agent gets with returned findings. */
+export function findingsMessage({ kind, round, returned, how }) {
+  return [
+    `[code_boss: Checking your work (${kind}, round ${round}) found what follows. Fix what holds up, check it again (run the tests, the reproduction), then ${how} again - it is checked again. What does not hold up, say why in your reply.]`,
+    ...returned.map(fLine),
+  ].join('\n');
+}
+// THE DEVELOPER'S BOXES ARE IN THE AGENT'S VOICE (the developer, 2026-11-07: "if there's feedback ... it reads like
+// it's the agent either talking to the user or thinking out loud"; 2026-11-06: "not that there are 2 agents"): "my
+// plan", "I", "you" - no "the check of the agent's", no impact/confidence labels (those are code_boss's, for the gate)
+const MY = { plan: 'my plan', design: 'my plan', change: 'my change', diagnosis: 'my diagnosis', risky: 'the step I am about to take' };
+const myOf = (kind) => MY[kind] || 'my work';
+const devLine = (f) => `- ${f.unsure || f.confidence === 'low' ? 'I am not sure of this one: ' : ''}${f.what}${f.evidence ? `\n  How I know: ${f.evidence}` : ''}`;
+/** What is shown in place of the work, said: a risky step is not taken, a feature's plan not built; the rest is shown. */
+const asShown = (kind) => kind === 'risky' ? 'I have not taken the step.' : kind === 'design' ? 'I have not started building it.' : `I have shown you ${myOf(kind)} as I had it.`;
+// A DESCRIPTION STANDS ON ITS OWN: "Add to conversation" quotes it into the message box without its title (live,
+// 2026-11-07: "> It is not checked. What I was about to do:" - it what?), and the box's own buttons say what they do
+/** The Heads up box once the agent has had its rounds, or when the review gave up (its findings, all of them, listed). */
+export function headsUpNote({ kind, round, returned = [], gaveUp = '', minor = [] }) {
+  const rest = minor.length ? `\n\n${gaveUp ? 'What I found:' : 'And some smaller things I did not go back for:'}\n${minor.map(devLine).join('\n')}` : '';
+  if (gaveUp) return { title: `I could not finish checking ${myOf(kind)}`, description: `I could not finish checking ${myOf(kind)}. ${gaveUp}${rest}\n\n${asShown(kind)}` };
+  return {
+    title: `${myOf(kind).replace(/^./, (c) => c.toUpperCase())} still has open points after ${round} checks`,
+    description: `I have checked ${myOf(kind)} and gone back to it ${round - 1} times already. What I still find:\n\n${returned.map(devLine).join('\n')}${rest}\n\n${asShown(kind)}`,
+  };
+}
+/**
+ * WHAT IS BELOW THE BAR IS NOT THROWN AWAY (the developer, 2026-11-06: "instead of throwing away a response if it's low
+ * impact or low confidence, let's show it as a heads up that the dev can optionally use"): a box of its own.
+ */
+export function minorNote({ kind, minor }) {
+  return {
+    title: `Small things I noticed checking ${myOf(kind)} (${minor.length})`,
+    description: `Small things I noticed checking ${myOf(kind)} - each small, or I am not sure of it, so I did not hold things up for it. Yours to use or not:\n\n${minor.map(devLine).join('\n')}`,
+  };

=== add test/test-commit-modal-e2e.mjs
new c838440971ea9e9f0df60919
+/**
+ * THE COMMIT WINDOW, end to end through the real page (the developer, 2026-11-06: "I still seem to have issues w/ the
+ * commit modal. It would be nice if you could do some testing focusing on it"; "allow collapsing of commit suggestions
+ * and of files underneath"; "ask it for 1 commit at a time ... That way the user doesn't have to wait minutes to see
+ * even the first commit"; "it maybe be that the hanging correlates w/ the git suggestion call"). A stand-in model
+ * answers one commit per call - a file's changes - after a delay; the content filters are the shipped ones.
+ *   [1] the commits come one at a time: the first is shown while the next is asked for, Commit waits for the last
+ *   [2] a message being typed in when the next commit comes keeps its text and its cursor
+ *   [3] a commit folds to its message, and each file of its changes on its heading
+ *   [4] each commit shows its own changes
+ *   [5] another repo's window shows that repo's changes, not the last one's
+ *   [6] Commit makes the checked commits, leaves the rest, and the count goes down
+ *   [7] a big change (one hunk of 40,000 lines) is suggested without holding the server: it answers throughout
+ * Run: node test/test-commit-modal-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cmodal-home-'));
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '100000';
+process.env.CB_COMMIT_RECS_RETRY_MS = '600000';
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+
+// the stand-in model: one commit per call - the units of the first file it is shown
+let DELAY = 200;

+const adapter = {
+  async complete({ messages }) {
+    const text = (messages || []).map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+    if (/Choose ONLY the NEXT commit|Group the units into commits/.test(text)) {
+      recCalls.push(Date.now());
+      await sleep(DELAY);
+      const ids = [...new Set([...text.matchAll(/\[([^\]\s]+)\]/g)].map((m) => m[1]).filter((id) => /#\d+$/.test(id) || /\.[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(id)))];
+      const file = (ids[0] || '').split('#')[0];
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: JSON.stringify({ message: 'feat: change ' + file, units: ids.filter((id) => id.split('#')[0] === file) }) } }] };
+    }
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },

+ * THE SERVER STOPS ANSWERING: THE WINDOW SAYS WHAT THE CONTAINER KNOWS, AND CAN RESTART IT (the developer, 2026-11-06:

+ *   [1] the page is given the address and key; the server's own environment no longer holds them (nothing it starts inherits them)
+ *   [2] the manager reaches it and the window does not: said so, with a button that reloads the window
+ *   [3] it is not answering the manager either: said so, with when the container restarts it by itself, and a red
+ *       button to restart it now
+ *   [4] the button: the manager is asked (with the key), the line says it is being restarted, and once it is back a
+ *       note says it was restarted and where what held it was written
+ * Run: node test/test-conn-restart-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import http from 'node:http';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-connrs-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const PORT = 29481 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);
+const KEY = 'ab'.repeat(18);
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-connrs-proj-'));
+await mkdir(path.join(proj, '.code_boss'), { recursive: true });
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Answered.' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+
+// the stand-in for the container's manager (Manager.java's /code-boss/): POST, the key as the body, answered for the page's origin
+let mstate = { id: 1, state: 'running', silentMs: 0, silentFor: '', autoRestartInMs: 0, restarting: false, restartWhy: '', restarts: 0, answering: true };
+const asked = [];
+let start = null, server = null;
+const mgr = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+  let body = ''; req.on('data', (d) => { body += d; }); req.on('end', async () => {
+    asked.push({ path: req.url, key: body, origin: req.headers.origin, type: req.headers['content-type'] });
+    if (body !== KEY) { res.writeHead(403); return res.end('{}'); }
+    const h = { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'access-control-allow-origin': req.headers.origin || '' };
+    if (req.url === '/code-boss/restart') {
+      mstate = { ...mstate, state: 'stopping', restarting: true, restartWhy: 'restarted from its window' };
+      setTimeout(async () => { ({ server } = await start()); mstate = { ...mstate, state: 'running', restarting: false, restarts: 1, answering: true, silentMs: 0, silentFor: '' }; }, 2500);
+    }
+    res.writeHead(200, h); res.end(JSON.stringify(mstate));
+  });
+});
+await new Promise((r) => mgr.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
+const MGR = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + mgr.address().port;
+
+start = () => { process.env.CB_MANAGER_URL = MGR; process.env.CB_RESTART_KEY = KEY; return startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj }); };
+({ server } = await start());

=== add test/test-held-watch.mjs
new 4ea53ac7cf65449a9431238d
+/**
+ * WHAT HELD THE SERVER (src/agent/held-watch.mjs): a second thread notices the server's thread has stopped running and
+ * writes to diag.log how long, what it was serving, and its stack.
+ *   [1] held in JavaScript (a loop): the line names the request and the function holding it
+ *   [2] held in a call that waits outside JavaScript (a child process run synchronously): said so at once, and when it
+ *       came back (no stack: one taken then is wherever it went next, not what held it)
+ *   [3] a thread that keeps running writes nothing; CB_HELD_WATCH=0 starts nothing
+ * Run: node test/test-held-watch.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { startHeldWatch } from '../src/agent/held-watch.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const dir = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-held-'));
+const read = (p) => (existsSync(p) ? readFileSync(p, 'utf8') : '');
+
+function holdTheThread(ms) { const end = Date.now() + ms; let x = 0; while (Date.now() < end) x++; return x; }
+
+console.log('\n[1] held in JavaScript');
+{
+  const diag = path.join(dir, 'diag-1.log');
+  const w = startHeldWatch({ diagPath: diag, heldMs: 1500, build: 't' });
+  const tick = setInterval(() => w.beat(), 200);
+  await sleep(500);
+  w.doing('POST /api/commit-apply');
+  holdTheThread(4500);
+  await sleep(1500);
+  const said = read(diag);
+  ok(/\[held\] the server has not run for \d+s \(build t, pid \d+\) - the last request it took: POST \/api\/commit-apply/.test(said), 'the line says how long and what it was serving');
+  ok(/its stack: holdTheThread test-held-watch\.mjs:\d+/.test(said), `and names the function holding it (${(said.match(/its stack: [^\n]{0,80}/) || [''])[0]})`);
+  clearInterval(tick); w.stop();
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] held in a call that waits outside JavaScript');
+{
+  const diag = path.join(dir, 'diag-2.log');
+  const w = startHeldWatch({ diagPath: diag, heldMs: 1500, build: 't' });
+  const tick = setInterval(() => w.beat(), 200);
+  await sleep(500);
+  w.doing('POST /api/commit-apply');
+  function waitOnAChild() { return spawnSync(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setTimeout(() => {}, 8000)']); }
+  waitOnAChild();
+  await sleep(1500);
+  const said = read(diag);
+  ok(/inside a call that waits outside JavaScript/.test(said), 'said while it was held: it cannot be paused there');
+  ok(/it came back to JavaScript \d+s after the hold began/.test(said), `and when it came back - not a stack taken after it had moved on (${(said.match(/came back[^\n]{0,60}/) || [''])[0]})`);
+  ok(!/its stack:/.test(said), 'no stack is given for it');
+  clearInterval(tick); w.stop();
+}

=== add test/test-review-loop-e2e.mjs
new c638a74d53b6f52747ef2261
+/**
+ * CHECKING MY WORK, through the real server (REVIEW-LOOP-SPEC.md): a scripted main agent says it is done - done() or
+ * commit() - and a scripted reviewer (a sub-agent of role 'review') checks it.
+ *   [A] a plan, passed: the reviewer reads the main conversation (mainChat), the agent's text is shown, the turn is the developer's
+ *   [B] a change sent back, fixed, checked again (round 2: given round 1's findings), passed: the certify text shown
+ *   [C] a diagnosis that keeps failing: rounds 1-3 go back to the agent, round 4 goes to a Heads up box and the text is shown
+ *   [D] a reviewer that gives up: a Heads up box with its text, and the agent's text shown
+ *   [E] round 3 with no reason it is not looping: a failure - a Heads up box, nothing more to the agent
+ *   [F] a risky step, passed: the agent is told to go ahead (nothing is shown for it)
+ *   [G] nothing of the reviewer's own conversation reaches the main agent; the cards are on the screen's side only
+ *   [Q] its reply after handing to a check is tags only (as live): no "came back without a written reply" anywhere
+ * Run: node test/test-review-loop-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rloop-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '100000';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const textOf = (req) => (req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : Array.isArray(m.content) ? m.content.map((p) => p.text || '').join('\n') : '')).join('\n');
+const mainReqs = [], reviewReqs = [];
+let CASE = '';   // (the case being run: a findings message does not name it)
+const js = (code) => `<remote-workspace><js>${code}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+function mainReply(full) {
+  const nonce = [...full.matchAll(/--- you ([a-z0-9]{4}) ---/g)].pop()?.[1] || 'ab12';
+  const C = `/--- you ${nonce} ---`;
+  const latest = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf('\n--- developer'));
+  const has = (s) => latest.includes(s);
+  // (as the live model answered after done(), 2026-11-06: tags only - nothing of its own to say)
+  if (has('<remote-workspace-result')) return '<no-hand-over/>\n\n' + C + '\n\n<keywords>check, plan</keywords>';
+  if (has('[code_boss: Checking the step you are about to take found nothing')) return 'Going ahead with the step: STEP-TAKEN.\n' + C;
+  if (has('[code_boss: Checking your plan found nothing')) return 'Building it now: BUILDING melons.\n' + C;
+  if (has('[code_boss: Checking your work (diagnosis') && CASE === 'figs') return 'Looked again.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "DIAG figs: it is the queue", brief: "figs again" });');
+  if (has('[code_boss: Checking your work (diagnosis')) return 'Looked again.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "DIAG bananas: it is the cache", brief: "bananas again" });');
+  if (has('[code_boss: Checking your work (change')) return 'Fixing it.\n' + js('await write("grapes.txt", "one\\ntwo\\n"); return await commit({ message: "fix grapes", complexity: 1, review: "the fix for what was found", certify: "CERTIFY grapes again", files: ["grapes.txt"] });');
+  if (has('apples')) return 'My plan.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "PLAN apples: the cause and the fix. Can you find anything wrong with this?", brief: "apples brief" });');
+  if (has('grapes')) return 'Adding it.\n' + js('await write("grapes.txt", "one\\n"); return await commit({ message: "add grapes", complexity: 5, review: "grapes: a new file", certify: "CERTIFY grapes", files: ["grapes.txt"] });');
+  if (has('bananas')) return 'The cause.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "DIAG bananas: it is the cache", brief: "bananas" });');
+  if (has('cherries')) return 'The cause.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "DIAG cherries: it is the timer", brief: "cherries" });');
+  if (has('figs')) return 'The cause.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "DIAG figs: it is the queue", brief: "figs" });');
+  if (has('dates')) return 'Before I delete it.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "risky", show: "delete the old dates table", brief: "dates" });');

+}
+const F = (impact, confidence) => ({ what: `REVIEWER-FINDING ${impact}/${confidence}`, impact, confidence, evidence: 'src/x.js:4 - REVIEWER-EVIDENCE' });
+const REV = (o) => 'REVIEWER-ONLY-WORDS checked it.\nREVIEW: ' + JSON.stringify(o) + '\nSTATUS: DONE';
+function reviewReply(full) {
+  // (its own last turn: the prompt itself holds examples of a result block)
+  const latest = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf('\n--- developer'));
+  if (!latest.includes('<remote-workspace-result')) return 'Reading the conversation.\n' + js('return await mainChat(6);');
+  // (judged by its own task only: what mainChat() brought in holds the earlier cases' words too)
+  const a = full.indexOf("CHECK THE WORK IN THE MAIN CONVERSATION"), b = full.indexOf('WHAT TO LOOK FOR', a);
+  full = full.slice(a, b > a ? b : undefined);
+  if (/PLAN apples/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'pass', findings: [F('low', 'high')] });
+  if (/CERTIFY grapes again/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'pass', findings: [] });
+  if (/CERTIFY grapes/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'retry', findings: [F('high', 'high')], notLooping: 'NOT-LOOPING: the first check of this change' });
+  if (/DIAG bananas/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'retry', findings: [F('high', 'high')], notLooping: 'NOT-LOOPING: still the cache, the last fix did not touch it' });
+  if (/DIAG cherries/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'give-up', findings: [], headsUp: 'GIVE-UP-TEXT: the timer is not in this project.' });
+  if (/DIAG figs/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'retry', findings: [F('high', 'high')] });   // (never a reason: rounds 1-2 go back anyway, round 3 fails)
+  if (/old dates table/.test(full)) return REV({ verdict: 'pass', findings: [] });
+  return REV({ verdict: 'pass', findings: [] });
+}
+let REVIEW_DELAY = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const full = textOf(req);
+    const isReview = full.includes("CHECK THE WORK IN THE MAIN CONVERSATION");
+    (isReview ? reviewReqs : mainReqs).push(full);
+    if (isReview && REVIEW_DELAY) await sleep(REVIEW_DELAY);
+    const body = isReview ? reviewReply(full) : mainReply(full);
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-rloop-'));

+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });

+const settled = async (ms = 60000) => {

+const after = (evs, n) => evs.slice(n);

+/**
+ * CHECKING MY WORK, AT ITS EDGES AND IN VOLUME (REVIEW-LOOP-SPEC.md; the developer, 2026-11-06: "please for now just do
+ * edge case/volume testing on the current version"). Through the real server, scripted main agent and reviewer:
+ *   [1] two checks started in one snippet: both run, both come to their end, both texts shown
+ *   [2] a reviewer that fails, one with no REVIEW line, one with a REVIEW line that is not JSON: each a Heads up box,
+ *       the agent's text shown, nothing sent to the agent
+ *   [3] the developer writes while a check runs: their message is answered first; the findings come after, in a run
+ *       of their own
+ *   [4] 100 findings in one answer: what reaches the agent and the Heads up box stays a readable size
+ *   [5] a 60 KB message to show: shown whole
+ *   [6] done() wrongly called, and where there is no check (the side chat): refused or told plainly
+ *   [7] a Heads up box from a check: "Add to conversation" and Dismiss are taken
+ *   [8] volume: 40 checks of 6 steps - the main agent's request does not grow with them, the conversation file
+ *       stays sane, and the page draws them (folded) quickly
+ *   [9] code_boss restarted while a check runs: the check does not stay "running" forever, and the agent's text is
+ *       not lost
+ * Run: node test/test-review-loop-edges.mjs
+ */

+function mainReply(full) {
+  const nonce = [...full.matchAll(/--- you ([a-z0-9]{4}) ---/g)].pop()?.[1] || 'ab12';
+  const C = `/--- you ${nonce} ---`;
+  const latest = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf('\n--- developer'));
+  const has = (s) => latest.includes(s);
+  if (has('<remote-workspace-result')) return 'Handed to the check.\n' + C;
+  if (has('[code_boss: Checking your work')) return 'I will look at those findings later.\n' + C;
+  if (has('meanwhile what time is it')) return 'MEANWHILE-ANSWER: it is late.\n' + C;
+  if (has('two at once')) return 'Both.\n' + js('await done({ kind: "plan", show: "TWO-PLAN shown", brief: "a" }); return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "TWO-DIAG shown", brief: "b" });');
+  if (has('a broken check')) return 'Plan.\n' + js(`return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "BROKEN-${CASE} shown", brief: "${CASE}" });`);
+  if (has('a slow check')) return 'Plan.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "SLOW-PLAN shown", brief: "slow" });');
+  if (has('a hundred findings')) return 'Plan.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "HUNDRED-PLAN shown", brief: "hundred" });');
+  if (has('a very long plan')) return 'Plan.\n' + js(`return await done({ kind: "plan", show: ${JSON.stringify(BIG)}, brief: "big" });`);
+  if (has('call it wrongly')) return 'Trying.\n' + js('const out = []; for (const a of [{}, { kind: "change", show: "x" }, { kind: "plan" }, { kind: "plan", show: "" }]) { try { await done(a); out.push("no error"); } catch (e) { out.push(String(e.message).slice(0, 120)); } } return out;');
+  if (has('volume check')) return 'Plan.\n' + js(`return await done({ kind: "diagnosis", show: "VOLUME-${CASE} shown", brief: "volume" });`);
+  if (has('an old plan')) return 'Plan.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "OLD-PLAN shown", brief: "old" });');
+  if (has('change the number')) return 'Noted.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "NEW-PLAN shown", brief: "new" });');
+  if (has('a check that hangs')) return 'Plan.\n' + js('return await done({ kind: "plan", show: "HANG-PLAN shown", brief: "hang" });');
+  return 'ok\n' + C;
+}
+async function reviewReply(full, signal) {
+  const latest = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf('\n--- developer'));
+  const steps = (latest.match(/<remote-workspace-result/g) || []).length ? (full.match(/Reading the conversation/g) || []).length : 0;
+  // (the plan in its own task: the new plan's check reads the old one in the conversation)
+  if (hangOld && /OLD-PLAN/.test(full.slice(full.indexOf('CHECK THE WORK IN THE MAIN CONVERSATION'), full.indexOf('WHAT TO LOOK FOR')))) { await new Promise((_, rej) => { if (signal) signal.addEventListener('abort', () => rej(new Error('aborted')), { once: true }); }); }
+  if (hangReview && /HANG-PLAN/.test(full)) { await new Promise((_, rej) => { if (signal) signal.addEventListener('abort', () => rej(new Error('aborted')), { once: true }); }); }
+  if (!latest.includes('<remote-workspace-result') || steps < REVIEW_STEPS) return `Reading the conversation, step ${steps + 1}.\n` + js('return await mainChat(3);');
+  const a = full.indexOf("CHECK THE WORK IN THE MAIN CONVERSATION"), b = full.indexOf('WHAT TO LOOK FOR', a);
